# Corpus index (ADR 0019)

Generated by `make corpus-export` (also `make corpus-status`). Do not edit by hand; edit `manifest.yaml`.
One row per manifest entry, sorted by source then doc_id. The spreadsheet version is `CORPUS_INDEX.xlsx`.

**Real available: 1084 / 500** (BRD G2). Distance to 500: **0**. Synthetic KFS and policies are not counted.

**manual**: Yes = manual source or placeholder; No = fetched or queued by script; Partial = script entry that needs a person. **status**: ok / ok_manual (counted), rejected (scan or pre-2015), needs_date (no usable date yet, including dates under review), pending.
